# Index of routes tried to close row D

Index of everything tried toward the sign (Weil positivity on the declared carrier, row D), across the
nine previous programs (01-context/RH1–RH9), phases 0–189 of 03-research, Papers 46–47, the master grid,
the crossword, and the literature (arXiv sweep, 68 approaches). About 1,400 entries in total. **Rule: nothing
listed here is retried.**

## Files

| File | Scope | Entries |
|---|---|---|
| `01_PROGRAMS_RH1-RH5.md` | Resonance, ω classes, extremes, covariance, inverse spectral (2017–2021) | 51 |
| `02_PROGRAMS_RH6-RH9.md` | Localized Weil form, Q detector, stability/monotonicity, Lefschetz/Arakelov (literature) | 38 |
| `03_PHASES_000-099.md` | Named walls (MW-*, NG-*, CIR-*, N5–N7), ES-1…27 record, phases 0–99 | ≈190 |
| `04_PHASES_100-189.md` | Curated Part I (W-* walls, T1–T5, Paper 47 barriers, falsifiers, phases 185–189); Part II: 824 grid routes | ≈1,025 |
| `05_LITERATURE.md` | Literature and arXiv sweep (L01–L68), with corpus coverage | 68 |
| `05b_LITERATURE_EXTENSION.md` | Sweep extension (L69–L85) and survey (L86–L110): arXiv, Zenodo, HAL, SSRN, academia.edu, alphaXiv, GitHub, OpenAlex | 42 |
| `06_OPEN_RESIDUES_DEAD_SUBROUTES.md` | For each live residue: statement, already-dead mini-routes, remaining directions and their barriers | 10 + ≈30 |
| `AUDIT_A.md`, `AUDIT_B.md` | Hostile audits of 2602.20313, 2609.02882/15329, Zenodo 23238720, 23177438, 19546495 | — |

Schema of each row: route · tool family · where · result (DEAD / NO-GO proved with class / FALSIFIED /
PARTIAL / NUMERICAL-ONLY / CIRCULAR / REF-D = ends in a reformulation of D) · why it failed.
Note: in Part II of `04_` the families were assigned by keyword (approximate); those in Part I are curated.

- `SURVEY_MODERN_UNTRIED.md` — survey of untried modern mathematics (seeds, nothing proved); top 5: real/hyperbolic stability by prime blocks, Bakry–Émery Γ₂, proof mining, modular bootstrap, resurgence.

## Closed families (do not retry without a new, explicit reason)

- **Statistics and numerical phenomenology** of Dirichlet polynomials: resonance, ω classes, extreme values, TDA,
  classifiers (RH1–RH4, RH6–RH8). Falsified or purely numerical; "multiplicativity suppresses resonance" is refuted
  by Liouville.
- **Inverse spectral / Hilbert–Pólya / Jacobi / Mercer** (RH5, phases 0–99, literature L44–L47): closed.
- **Li coefficients, Keiper–Li, Bochner kernels in ordinates**: blind to Re ρ or to feasible N.
- **Heat flow / de Bruijn–Newman / Laguerre–Pólya / Jensen**: dynamic NO-GO N5 (phase 8), circular (phase 28),
  heat trace DEAD (R164_502). Literature: the DBN kernel is not PF₅ (2602.20313).
- **Total positivity / PF / Karlin / Sturm / nodal measure**: 104_21 (fails PF₂), 104_83, R155_046/047,
  R162_1701 §2.4, R186_041/113.
- **Baker / linear forms in logarithms**: phases 12, 168 (too weak; NG-B7).
- **Fredholm / determinants / W-det / Birman–Schwinger**.
- **Dual, lattice, flat-top, SOS, exterior certificates** (Paper 47 §3–6, thm:Z, thm:R, latedge, N_019, N_020):
  the dual class is D; pure square arithmetic is empty.
- **Sieves, arithmetic progressions, short intervals, Bombieri–Vinogradov, Brun–Titchmarsh**: W-sieve, phase 167,
  N_021 Thm 2 (every class closed under finite modification dies by cor:void).
- **Zero information** (zero-free regions, density, verified zeros, zero geometry): cor:void C_zeros,
  thm:finZ, NT_011, NT_014.
- **Family of Dirichlet characters / averaging over the family**: R162_1102 Thm O.
- **Sonin / Connes / prolate / semilocal**: phase 183, R186; no sign.
- **Function fields, Hodge, Arakelov, Deligne, Laumon, F₁, arithmetic site**: phases 135, 145, 149, RH9; the
  object (surface) is missing, no closure.
- **Coercivity, Schur, bundle/inertia, Wiener–Hopf, Nyquist, two sectors, witnesses**: walls W-lin, W-det, W-fam, W-pat,
  W-loc, W-mom, W-coh, flowdet, margin.
- **Euler/Möbius algebra, operator partitions, orders and cones in 𝔄_T**: meta-theorem N_018 (identities +
  Bochner/Cauchy–Schwarz), N_007/008, L2_013/017.
- **Transport in σ, reflection of the functional equation, conjugation, Frobenius dilation**: L2_016, L2_025–027.
- **Integer lattice matrices A_M, balanced subspaces, separated multiplicative sets**: L2_028–031 (reformulation
  or trivial).
- **Height flow on ℚ_{>0}** (phase 189): organized; the pointwise part is zero geometry and part (b) is a
  zero-free strip.
- **Phase 194 (invention; detail below):** arithmetic Lévy structure / Hamburger rigidity (INV_A);
  pair surface 𝒮, Hodge/CS/nef/PRR-log (INV_B); pair counts, Farey, coprimes, Möbius, "h⁰ off the
  diagonal" (INV_C, Thm 4: no measure on log ℚ gives 𝔞_T); Mp₂ Weil character (INV_D); Kronecker–Hurwitz /
  Eichler–Selberg (INV_E); cones across ∂log (INV_F, H_cone); min/hyperbola balance term (INV_G);
  additive ℕ inputs, Ramanujan, Vaughan, divisor gauge (INV_H, INV_K: every gauge with Σ|φ|log d·d^{−½}<∞ is empty);
  coefficient-by-coefficient integrality (INV_I, H_mono); additive prime correlations, Hardy–Littlewood, sieves,
  pair correlation/Montgomery–Dyson, P36-4 (INV_J, H_sparse). Audits AUDIT_194(_2).md.

## Specification of the sign carrier (phase-194 synthesis, ARGUED as a set; each clause with its label)

Every candidate object must, simultaneously: (1) not be linear in Λ nor comb-generic (B″, G1, H; must fail for Beurling);
(2) not be a cone condition on log ζ (F2, PROVED); (3) not be coefficient-by-coefficient integrality (I2, PROVED);
(4) not be sparse-stable (J3, PROVED); (5) couple Λ at n<e^{2T} to the functional equation through a route other than
the factor ζ(½+iτ) (J4 ARGUED, K1 PROVED) — **OPEN, no known object**; (6) cross ∂log without losing positivity
(C Identity 5, E2 ARGUED, F). Pending line-by-line review: K2's own step (aligning p≤X aligns d X-smooth).

## Live residues (the only open ones)

(N)/(N_loc) as a correlation inequality on P_T; (Ω₀), (Ω_{t₀}), (Ω_Φ), (E_γ); the lower half of the two-sided
law; O166.802.1; the §28.4 object; the **near-coincidence part** of [Ξ(log(m/k))] (Paper 47, problem (S));
and the NT gate: an inequality at σ = 1 that uses multiplicativity and is not a zero-free-strip statement;
clause (5) of the sign-carrier specification (phase 194).

## Audits (results)

- **2602.20313 (Michalowski):** the de Bruijn–Newman kernel is not PF₅ (certified numerics, no arbitrage, CITED).
  Kills every route that needs PF_k / total positivity of the kernel with k ≥ 5; PF₄ open, do not assume it.
- **2609.02882 (Lamzouri), 2609.15329 (Pearce-Crump):** zero proportions on the line, not inertia; no sign per
  window. Alpöge–Furman: consistent with Theorem P, does not improve it.
- **Zenodo 23238720 (Johns, nic410):** LP over the whole line, parallel to Paper 47's duality; its κ* is not our
  κ_⋆. **Open candidate tool:** its primal pairwise-floor method (check against W4 before use).
- **Zenodo 23177438 (Eberwein):** weaker duplicates of our walls; blind-zone depth formula
  usable as a lemma. "Lemke 2026" has no public text.
- **Zenodo 19546495 (Franchi Viceré):** NO-GO, two fatal errors (rules out the rest of the semilocal track; "QW_S ≥ 0 ∀S finite" is
  false at S = {∞}).

## Phase 190: experiment on the near-coincidence matrix (R9) and (Ω₀) (R2)

`03-research/phase-190-near-coincidence-experiment/`. NUMERICAL (discovery, double precision, verified convergence).
- A_M(Λ) ⪰ 0 for M ≤ 3000, T₀ ∈ {0.05,0.15,0.3}; λ_min ≍ M^{−3+o(1)} (criticality law; no contact with the sign).
- 24 indefinite controls for M ≤ 400 (a₃, a₅, Beurling, Poisson, M₁ already at M=50; perturbation ±1e-5 of v₂ from M≈200–400).
- **No ζ-specific fact from the prime side.** All the statistics of Near (norm ≈0.17M, full rank, 56% positive
  entries, mass ∝ Λ(n)n^{−3/2}, orthogonal to the negative Arch cone, flat in h=m−kn) are identical to 5 digits for the
  1e-5 perturbations → not specific by [sens]. The minimizer is m^{−½}·w(m/M) (smoothed partial sum of ζ(½+iτ)), zero-side.
- Identity C3 (PROVED, elementary, class W7): sᵀ(Div+Near)s = 2Σ_N N^{−½}log N·E_M(N) over s=m^{−½}; the balance against Arch
  is carried only by the defects at N=2,3,4; every atom n≥5 cancels its Div (a Riemann-sum fact shared by all combs).
- (Ω₀): holds for Λ at T∈[0.55,1.10] (margin 0.016–0.091); it is only necessary (amp2 and ±1e-2 pass it).
- **New dead routes:** statistics/spectrum of Near as a fingerprint of ζ (NOT specific, [sens]); (Ω₀) as a filter (necessary, weak);
  more M / more controls do not change the classification.
- **Residue it leaves (the only step that follows):** Q_M(w)=s_wᵀA_M s_w over the family s_w=m^{−½}w(m/M) — split with C3 into
  a bulk part (defects N=2,3,4 vs. archimedean energy, positive with margin for flat w) and a boundary part at scale M; decide
  whether the boundary is PNT-error size ([void] → REF-D) or margin size M^{−3} (a new inequality on Λ at scale M).

## Phase 191: T* design round — two specifications, both NOT ADMISSIBLE

`03-research/phase-191-design-T-star/`. No proofs or numerics; clause-by-clause verdict against the shape theorem
(Paper 47 §20, Thm 20.28, Problem (R)) and (α)–(θ).
- **SPEC_A, exact induction on T by contacts:** NOT ADMISSIBLE. Fails (γ) (the step inherits λ_A(T_{j+1}) ≈ exp(−13e^{2T}),
  doubly exponential, against any saving exp(−2δT)), B8/(δ) (L* is an exact reformulation), (θ) (ℤ only as data),
  A15 if fed with BV/sieves. Useful design correction: a prime's entry instant is empty (lem:contA, R186_048);
  the new atom is only visible through a 2ε collar at the boundary; the residual conjecture C*_A is a property of the **outer
  field of the minimizer** h*_j (small to precision √μ_j), not of Λ = wall "functional of h*, not of σ" (phases 183–186).
- **SPEC_B, individual estimator with sign at a selected frequency:** NOT ADMISSIBLE in its three formalizations:
  T*₁ (one frequency) = exact (E_γ), no slack (B2b), one γ does not decide the window (λ), content = summability in the
  half-plane (A17); T*₂ (Nyquist grid) pointwise is FALSE for ζ (U_T≠∅) and correlated is (N) ⟺ D (B8); T*₃(η) (family
  s_w=m^{−½}w(m/M)) PROVED as an identity: the comb enters only through Λ∗1=linear log (W7) and Λ(2),Λ(3),Λ(4) → it is D on
  the integer lattice with the comb collapsed; it does not contribute (γ).
- **Conclusion of the round (new candidate clause for the shape theorem):** the admissible object **cannot be an
  estimate** ("bound on Λ + margin"): every prime estimate operates at scale exp(−cT) and the sign lives at scale
  exp(−e^{2T}). T*, if it exists, has to be an **equality or exact structure** in which the margin is not spent.

## Phase 192: construction round — exact identities (EI_001–EI_003)

`03-research/phase-192-exact-identity/`. Pencil and paper; no numerics; no certified sign; no negative value for ζ.
- **EI_001** outer field of the minimizer: E = (boundary polar moment) − e^{−t/2}(Chebyshev fluctuation at scale e^t tested by h*) − (archimedean
  tail exp. 5/2), PROVED; Ê = (σ_T − λ)ĥ* − Tate with σ_T meromorphic with no poles at the zeros (FE). Linear in Λ at fixed h*; ℤ does not enter. C*_A unchanged.
- **EI_002** family s_w: Q = (1/π)∫F̂₀²|ζ_w|²Re R_{X′}; exact Mellin expansion ζ_w = cζ + M^{½−iτ}W + ρ_M (= Poisson on ℤ); Poisson on ℤ² with
  second-order cancellation of the (0,0) term in the bulk (both Tate conditions), corrected at the boundary; self-duality of near-coincidences;
  Fact N2 (phase 190) is upgraded to PROVED (descriptive). No square emerges (U_T ≠ ∅); the remaining Mellin term is not bounded under M^{−3}.
- **EI_003** Euler product beyond the window Z_X = ζ/Z_{<X}, phase Θ_X, window Hardy function Z_T: σ_T = 2Θ_X′, R_X = Θ_X′ + iZ_T′/Z_T.
  **Symbol rigidity theorem (NO-GO, PROVED):** no exact identity on σ_T built with FE, Euler, Mellin, Poisson or Riemann–Siegel is
  non-linear in Λ; non-linearity only enters through h*(Λ) (REF-D, Lemma S) or through tests built with the coefficients of ζ (twisted second moment).
- **Column bookkeeping:** "non-linear Λ" goes from YES to NO on the symbol side in all three objects; "doubly exponential margin from the
  identity" goes to NO in all three; "ℤ as mechanism" is retained only in EI_002/003 (test side). Residue: T*₃(η) / (S) over the family, OPEN.

## Phase 192: three exact identities on the minimizer — adversarial review: all three EXCLUDED

`03-research/phase-192-exact-identity/` (EI_001–003 + reviews + PHASE_192_REVIEW_SUMMARY.md). Pencil and paper. No contact
with the sign.
- **EI_001 (outer field of the minimizer):** three-term identity PROVED (= split of prop:wit-cheb outside I_T); its
  sign content is C*_A = Lemma S (REF-D). Correction endorsed to SPEC_A §2(d).
- **EI_002 (family s_w with Poisson on ℤ and ℤ²):** Thm 1–4 PROVED; its sign content is T*₃(η) = (S) (REF-D). Correction
  endorsed to SPEC_B Line 2 (second-order cancellation from the two Tate conditions in the bulk). **Rejected**: "Fact N2
  → PROVED" (false: at low ordinates the pole dominates; it remains NUMERICAL). The Poisson framework needs the cutoff y ≥ 1 (FIX).
- **EI_003 ("symbol rigidity"):** Thm 1 PROVED (σ_T = 2Θ_X′). **Thm 2 is a tautology**, not a NO-GO: σ_T is affine in Λ by
  definition; clause (R)(3) refers to the use of Λ in the derivation, not the symbol. Category error; ARGUED at most.
  Do NOT write P192-EI3 as a NO-GO.
- **Why all three are excluded:** each identity survives ALL the modifications O1–O15 applied to the comb (the
  reviewer applied them one by one); "fails by identification" is W-identification, excluded by thm:shape(C).
- **Column moves (YES→NO): none endorsed.** All construction proposals rejected. The admissible region
  (Problem (R)) remains as it was.
- **Lesson recorded:** an exact identity that holds for every comb cannot carry the sign; what would make it specific to
  ζ is exactly what was not written.

## Correction (closure of the shape theorem, Paper 47 §20, 159 pp.)

**The scale of the margin was overstated in the phase 191–192 sections of this README.** What is PROVED is
λ_A(T) ≤ 4·10⁹·T·e^{−15.449T} (thm:margin, cor:margin1): simple exponential. The doubly exponential scale exp(−e^{(2−ε)T})
or exp(−13e^{2T}) is only Zhu's conjectural law / the NUMERICAL pattern from phase 187, and is not used in any proof. The
clause "it is not an estimate" remains in the paper as `lem:shape-est`: (i) PROVED with the correct margin bound; (ii)
exact Schur reductions with a finite complement inherit the margin (PROVED), with the infinite collar space (Lemma S)
ARGUED; (iii) CONDITIONAL on κ_T ≠ 0; (iv) the general reading "no estimate works" remains ARGUED, not PROVED.
"It is not an identity valid for every comb": `lem:shape-ident`, PROVED. Problem (R) gains clause (11): the sign step
has to be ζ-specific by construction.

## Phase 193, fronts C and D — 0 admissible; one retraction

- **Front C (local–global/adelic, then new areas):** the product formula as a coupling (relaxation table, PROVED: either it
  pins down Λ or there is a negative comb); infinite-dimensional MW-3 Hasse–Minkowski → NO-GO in the classical class (⟨1,1,1,1⟩ vs ⟨−1,…⟩,
  PROVED; the well-posed reading is (S)); rationality of ζ(2k)/π^{2k}, Kummer, Iwasawa (no real sign / rational
  quartets); o-minimality/Pila–Wilkie (empty: semialgebraic set; B′); free probability (they commute; criticality
  invisible to the limit distribution). **RETRACTION (CA_C_005):** the "M^{−3} criticality of the family s_w with η>2/3" was a
  normalization artifact (‖s‖² instead of ‖F‖²=sᵀGs); in the correct norm η≥0.7 has growing margin, only the full form
  and η≈0.4 are critical, and that criticality is on the zero side. Family η>e^{2T₀}/3: reduction to a Schur scalar PROVED;
  positive for every object with proved FE (incl. Epstein x²+5y², whose full form is NEGATIVE), negative without FE (Beurling)
  → it is not a sign step (O10). NUMERICAL data point: the full form separates ζ and ζ_{ℚ(i)} (critical, positive) from Epstein x²+5y².
- **Front D (phase / Hardy function):** Hardy amplitude transfer (Thm 1/1′/1″ PROVED; holds for every series with FE and a
  simple pole → a tool, not a sign); unconditional mean-value inequality ∫|F̂|²|ζ|²e^{−2C_X} ≥ ∫|F̂|² on P_T (PROVED);
  amplitude-weighted tests do not approximate P_T (PROVED). Landau/maximum with z_X≥0 (retry of R164_102), Kramers–Kronig,
  causality, de Branges/Kreĭn, Riemann–Siegel chirp: dead. Residue: D lives in {|ζ/Z_{<X}|<1}, the central Nyquist cell
  (= (R)(7), R9).

## GUIDING PRINCIPLE (marked by the author as vital)

**The sign has to come from the semiring (ℕ, +, ·): from the interaction between sum and product.** A purely
multiplicative property is not enough (G1, PROVED: the free monoid ℕ^× is indistinguishable from Beurling and from the ideal
monoids of any field → survives O11/O12); a purely additive one is not enough either (comb-generic / generic FE-class: Poisson
over ℤ + Γ holds for Epstein, CA_C_007). The near-coincidences m ≈ kn (residue R9, problem (S)) are exactly the place where
additive proximity meets multiplicative structure. Geometric side (CA_F_003, gap G-F): a ℤ-polarized object whose
positivity comes from amplitude/Riemann–Roch and not from the signature of 𝔞_T — in Weil over 𝔽_q it is C×C, where + and · interact via
Frobenius. **Every new seed must state how it uses the +/· interaction and why it is not purely a monoid property nor purely additive.**

## Phase 193, fronts E, F, G, H — 0 admissible

- **E (quantum / operator algebras):** Thm 1 separability (D ⟺ positivity over Euler product vectors; positive =
  completely positive, PROVED) → quantum information (Choi, witnesses, NC-SOS) adds nothing; Thm 2: Bochner measure on the
  solenoid concentrated on the Kronecker line ι(ℝ); no Schur factorization by product kernels (PROVED). KMS beyond
  Bost–Connes, Connes cocycle, Jones, CCR/Fock, ITPFI/Kakutani, Golden–Thompson, Araki/Petz, operator systems: dead.
- **F (topology / geometry):** Floer (= λ_A, REF-D), Bridgeland (support = 𝔞_T≻0), A¹/RO(G)/motivic (signatures), Hodge–Riemann
  /Lorentzian (= parity Schur), MW-3 condensate/liquid (NO-GO PROVED), exact Selberg/Arthur traces (NO-GO PROVED class
  TF⁺), unitary invariants = spectral data (Hahn–Hellinger, PROVED). **Open gap G-F:** a ℤ-polarized object with an
  ample class, quadratic h⁰, and a trace property (= MW-5; a missing construction, not an impossibility).
- **G (exotic algebras / combinatorics):** G0 (squares = Gram factorizations → REF-D or certificate) and G1 (the free monoid
  ℕ^× does not distinguish ζ from Beurling nor from field monoids), PROVED. Supersymmetry, Clifford, Soergel/KL, VOA/moonshine, Lorentzian,
  cluster, Jordan, Connes–Kreimer, involutions, Elias–Williamson on the window ladder: dead.
- **H (semiring (ℕ,+,·)):** Buium p-derivations / arithmetic wronskian (fails on carrying; monoid), Stepanov over ℤ
  (retry of G1_B9), sum-product (estimate), arithmetic derivative (monoid); object polarized by global Frobenius
  lifts: NO-GO class 𝒦_δ (PROVED/CITED). Separating invariant (S⁺) (Erdős 1946): comb on prime powers with divisor sum
  continuous on successors ⟺ = cΛ — separates ζ from O1–O3,O5,O6,O8,O10–O12,O15 but not from O7. **Anatomy PROVED:** every
  term of the form is (a purely additive weight in log q) × (a coefficient purely from the monoid in q), linear in Λ; Near = Kronecker
  line minus Haar on the solenoid. **Consequence:** non-linearity can only come from the minimizer (REF-D) or from a
  new input that is neither the minimizer nor a property of the comb.

**Label precision for the GUIDING PRINCIPLE (adversarial review, rounds 10–11):** G1 ("the monoid sees nothing") excludes by (R)(10),
not by thm:shape(A); in Paper 47 the Beurling falsifier is ARGUED and the ζ_K one is OPEN. "Purely additive ⇒ shared by
Epstein x²+5y²" is ARGUED (that Epstein has Γ(s) and conductor 20). The principle remains a design guide with those labels, not
a theorem.

## Phase 193, fronts I and J — 0 admissible

- **I (analysis/PDE of the minimizer):** Markov structure of the form without the polar term (semigroup that improves positivity;
  λ₀ < λ_A ≤ λ₂, PROVED); NO-GO Thm 2: Markov/Perron–Frobenius/non-local maximum/parity/Tate do not decide (O2 preserves them, PROVED);
  **Thm 3 rigidity (PROVED, ξ(σ)>0 CITED):** every FE-symmetric rational twist with entire ξE (quartets, adding/removing zeros on the
  line) stops being Markov for T ≥ T_E; **Cor 6:** the set {(S⁺), pole residue, entire with FE and Γ_ℝ, Markov ∀T} separates ζ
  from every declared modification except O13 and O14 (open) — a separation, not an inequality (REF-D as a route). Virial/Pohozaev
  (holds for every comb, DH included), boundary layer (upper bound only), Almgren (frequency ≳ e^{(2−ε)T}), Γ-convergence,
  concentration–compactness, rearrangements: dead. Every F ∈ P_T has ≥ 2 sign changes (PROVED).
- **J (correlation inequalities):** chessboard / lattice reflection positivity in prime coordinates: NO-GO (PROVED;
  in two primes forces constant 𝖡); **frustration (PROVED, Thm 1.3):** no ±1 gauge makes 𝔸_M ferromagnetic — the
  additive cycle m→m+1→…→2m closed by the multiplicative edge (m,2m) is frustrated → GKS/Ginibre/GHS/Lebowitz/FK with no input; FKG on
  the divisor lattice: the FKG equality class is exactly the Euler product vectors (CA_E_001), so FKG says
  nothing; Brascamp–Lieb (REF-D or generic), Prékopa–Leindler (retry of PF), strong Rayleigh (Lee–Yang), Dobrushin/clusters
  (do not reach [crit]): dead. Reading of the guiding principle: + and · meet in two places and both are obstructions
  (monoid symmetries do not preserve the Kronecker line; additive/multiplicative frustration).

## Phase 193, front K — frustrated quadratic forms: 0 admissible

Diamagnetism on integer sites (|couplings| → −∞ version, PROVED); diamagnetism on the t-line: ζ is the non-frustrated
member, λ₀(T;a) ≥ λ₀(T;Λ) ∀|a|≤Λ, but on P_T the order reverses (**anti-diamagnetism**: adding the primes RAISES the background,
λ_A(0.7; no comb) < 0 < λ_A(0.7; Λ), with cor:Tarch and Zhu CITED) — the front's only ζ-specific fact, and it is an obstruction;
ℤ/2 holonomy of frustration = sgn 𝖡(log(m/k)), insufficient (counterexample g𝖡); signed Laplacian (Cheeger reaches ½,
1−O(1/M) is needed); Guerra/ASS interpolation (concave → REF-D); Grothendieck/SDP/Lasserre (G0/thm:sos); Talagrand (no
disorder); Lieb's flux-phase (needs reflection, dead); TAP (perturbative). **New exact tool — site independence
(PROVED, reformulation):** D ⟺ PSD 𝖡 matrices over any set of sites with consecutive ratios → 1; on
prime sites Div ≡ 0 and D becomes Arch ≥ Near over prime tests: the whole deficit is paid by Near via p ≈ n·p′.

## Phase 193, front L — exact magic constructions (Viazovska): 0 admissible

The sphere-packing method rests on (V1) discrete primal with exact attainment, (V2) Poisson combs positive on
both sides, (V3) Fourier eigenfunction structure via modular forms; row D lacks all three in the window LP.
**Magic function for the window form: NO-GO (PROVED, thm:O):** the primal optimum has support ℝ, complementary slackness
would force certificate ≡ λ_P, impossible. Flat-top certificate = smoothing of the zero side (thm:Z). Cohn–Elkies gives upper bounds
(Odlyzko direction). Constructions using only modular data: would certify Epstein x²+5y² (level 20, PROVED with the cited
falsifier); at level 1 they reduce to the comb. **Prime-site LP on the Kronecker torus (PROVED, REF-D):** dual attained, vanishing
set = minimizer (exact analogue of Viazovska's), exact for |X| ≤ 5 (ellipsoid-rank, CITED), but comb-generic.

## Phase 193, front M — problem on prime sites: 0 admissible. CORRECTS THE GUIDING PRINCIPLE

- **Exact form on prime sites (PROVED):** Near = Σ_h C_s(h), Chen-type correlations of p with p−h = p′q^a, over ALL
  shifts |h| ≲ 2T₀p; it encodes the first-moment Hardy–Littlewood for (x, qx+h), NOT twin primes, Goldbach, or k-tuples.
- **Site arithmetic carries no information (PROVED):** parity and residues are reindexings of Near = Σ_q Near_q (linear
  in Λ, each Near_q of trace 0 → indefinite). **Thm 3.1/Cor 3.2:** any real set of sites with ratios → 1 is decisive;
  the statement "∀M" on primes is equivalent to the same statement on sites p+θ_p with independent irrational θ_p, where x − nx′ is
  never an integer → parity, residues, singular series, Chen switching, sieve and parity DO NOT EVEN EXIST, and the truth value is the
  same. Bounded-shift configurations (twins/Chen) have weight 0 in the decisive configurations.
- **Anti-diamagnetism = exact cancellation (PROVED):** m(τ) + 2Re(ζ′/ζ)(½+iτ) = 0 for every real τ outside the ordinates of zeros
  (FE + reality of ξ; new for all τ, previously only τ=0). The primes contribute no surplus: they cancel C₀ exactly; it holds for
  the entire class with FE.
- **CORRECTION TO THE GUIDING PRINCIPLE:** the "+" in p − n·p′ is cosmetic. The only +/· content is that of the comb's
  positions log n with weights v_n (successor property (S⁺), CA_H_002), which is independent of the sites. The principle now reads: the sign
  would have to come from the structure of the comb on log ℕ (positions + weights) read through the form, without going through
  site arithmetic or symbol densities (which cancel exactly) — and everything that is not that belongs to the zero side.

`AUDIT_PHASE193_CERTAINTY.md` (~105 dismissals, phases 190–193). Conclusion: "0 admissible" holds only in the narrow sense
(no seed *as it was formulated* passes the proven filters); "these areas are closed" is NOT established: ~1/3 of the
dismissals are reformulations (REF-D = valid objectives, not closures), ~1/4 are one-liners or ARGUED, and ~8 depend only on a
weak clause (O11 ARGUED, O12 OPEN, G1→(R)(10), O10 full form only). No dismissal turned out to be false.
**Labels corrected in this README:** Lewis–Zagier route: NO-GO **ARGUED** (not proved); MW-3 condensate: NO-GO PROVED **only
for the three declared classes**; SPEC_A (phase 191): its death by (γ) used the already-retracted doubly exponential scale →
**reopened to be redone with the proved margin bound**. **Candidates to re-examine** (in order of plausibility): separating set
𝖯⋆ (CA_I_001 Cor 6); prime-site LP on the torus (CA_L_002); amplitude homotopy W(α) (D8, a one-line death); site
independence Arch ≥ Near and (S)/(N); residues from front A (Rankin–Selberg in w, metaplectic package, Salié/Kuznetsov); SPEC_A with
the proved margin; PF₄ (OPEN); paired vectors built from primes.

### Re-examinations
- **D8 amplitude homotopy W(α):** the one-line death was MISFORMULATED (the family W_F(α)=∫Θ′|F̂|²|Z_T|^{2α} is well
  defined with no zeros, PROVED); now dead by THEOREM: interpolation from the proved integers is NO-GO (model p−qe^{−2αL},
  PROVED); W(0) ≥ W(1) is pointwise false in the central cell (PROVED); α not an integer by the CA_D_001 mechanism → test
  class {0}. A ζ-specific input would have to act on {|Z_T|<1} = the central cell (REF-D).
- **SPEC_A redone with the proved margin:** step slack ≤ X^{−7.72} (≤ X^{−15.45} with the full budget) against savings
  δ ≤ ½ from VK/BV/MR/Selberg → (γ) still fails with the dominant power source (ARGUED, no longer circular). **The exact
  induction version USING ONLY IDENTITIES remains OPEN and ADMISSIBLE in (γ)/A15**; B8 is REF-D (a valid objective); its only gap is
  (θ): specifying where log ℕ enters ‖ℓ_e‖_{𝔄_j^{−1}} (e.g. the coincidence of the contact block v_{n_j} against 2n_j^{−½}
  at the 2T_j offset). → live candidate.
- **𝖯⋆ (CA_R1_001):** REF-D by identification, now PROVED (previously ARGUED): Markov criterion = jump measure J_T ≥ 0
  (cone condition, stable under adding positive mass); Markov does not bound the comb (O2 and (1+c)Λ are Markov ∀T with λ_A → −∞);
  every proper sub-conjunction of 𝖯⋆ has a falsifier; the clauses only force a = Λ and K ≡ 0. O14 closed: it is not an instance of 𝖯⋆
  (bounded form; breaks consistency under extension). **New OPEN residue: D_∃c** — ∀T ∃c>0 with λ_A(T; cΛ) ≥ 0 (nested
  intervals in T ⇒ equivalent to a common c*); D ⇒ D_∃c; the converse is question O4^ε (κ_T ≠ 0), OPEN.

- **INV_T_001 (lead T, phase 195; gauge residue of AUDIT_4):** Green identity with unimodular gauge I. T1 PROVED: only q=I_2/I_1 matters. It is admissible (recovers 𝔞_T exactly) ⟺ ∂_σlog|q| at σ=½ equals, in band, 2∂_σu_S, with ν̂ (frequencies ≥2T) and β·ĝ (over 𝒫_T) free: this is the (ν,β) freedom of def:PD. T2 PROVED: with I_1=e^{−λ(s−½)}, λ→∞, the flux at σ_1 vanishes and 𝔞_T is a pure energy defect (sign of the bulk ⟺ sign of the flux). ## Certified frontier

- **Certified positivity (CITED, computationally verified in AUDIT_ZENODO_FRONTIER.md): T ≤ ½log15 ≈ 1.354 (Mori, Zenodo 23281236, margin ≥ 2^{−2904}).** Surpasses Liu (17/16) and Paper 47 (½log5). Plausible quantitative ceiling (Mori 23175060/23363767): window minimum ≲ exp(−4πe^{2T}). Our Galerkin computations at T=1 (μ_even ≤ 2.5e−22) do NOT resolve the real background (~1e−29 according to the prolate model). New falsifier F-DH (Davenport–Heilbronn, a=9/5).

## Survey  (`00-references/survey/`, S1–S5 + addenda + SYNTHESIS.md; no adversarial audit)
- **Coverage:** ≈2,500 titles (S1 835, S2 ≈560, S3 455, S4 ≈390, S5 ≈810; OpenAlex + arXiv search; arXiv API with 429s), ≈75 in-depth readings. New entries L86–L110 in `05b_LITERATURE_EXTENSION.md`.
- **Verdict:** no item has all four properties P1–P4 of the BRIEF; no NEW-CANDIDATE survives. New NO-GOs: Bombieri–Garrett 2002.07929 (PROVED from the structure of the paper: FE-generic, serves only a single CM / Epstein point); Faddeev–Pavlov/Lax–Phillips (ARGUED: primes in weight Λ(n)/n and frequency 2log n); MSS (by type, ∃/∀; = INV_M_001). Knauf: OPEN-not-very-promising (positivity only for real β>2).
- **Certificate frontier:** Mori ½log15 (Zenodo 23281236, L104): computational part VERIFIED by us (AUDIT_ZENODO_FRONTIER.md), label CITED; Senke 77/64 (22871383) and Mori's upper bound (23175060) remain UNVERIFIED. [Updated, phase 200 L5-T2.]
- **Connection layer (SYNTHESIS §4; ARGUED):** (i) the Rankin–Selberg sign (|a_p|² ≥ 0) and FE rigidity (Hamburger/Selberg) share the same blind spot, Kronecker parity d(p^j)↦(−1)^j d(p^j) (X2 of BARRIER_X_001): combining them does not separate Λ from its twins at odd levels; only the residue of even levels p^{2j} < e^{2T} remains (already OPEN in X_001). (ii) **Weight gap:** every built-in positivity found reads the primes at weight ≤ n^{−1} or beyond absolute convergence (FP unitarity, Bombieri–Garrett, Ш/Poisson = Euler at σ=1 (V1.3), Rankin–Selberg σ>1, Knauf β>2, CCM local moments); the only positivity at weight n^{−1/2} is the multiplicative Hilbert matrix (Helson/Perfekt), without FE. The cell «weight ½ + global FE + built-in positivity» is empty in the literature. (iii) Wei (integer-mass quasicrystals) + Kaltenbäck–Woracek: does not apply (a.c. density of Γ_ℝ, signed comb; dead integer invariant, BARRIER_W_001).
- **Best-ranked connection candidate (OPEN, not promising):** semilocal CCM (local P1, P3, P4) + Guo Chen's residual identity via summation map (P2, identity) + cluster scale e^{−πλ²} as resolution restriction. Falls onto L81 («semilocal stabilization without a sign source») unless Chen's gluing term supplies the source; new reason written in SYNTHESIS §4 C1. Do not launch without checking L81/phase 183/INV_L_001.
- **Recommended cheap action:** F-DH gate with explicit window (Ziad, a = 9/5), certify in the def:PD convention and add to CERT_FALSIFIERS. Row D OPEN.
- **PATTERN_001 (phase 199, analyst; not a constructive route):** boundaries μ=e^{2T} ∈ {5, 8.37, 10, 12, 15} = endpoints (or interior points) of contact-free intervals; certificates piecewise between contacts (Lemma M, PROVED); margin ≈ κ(1−λ₄(2πμ)) ~ μ^{9/2}e^{−4πμ}, κ∈[4.1,5.4] (Mori V, NUMERICAL), measured slope −12.13 vs 4π; **contact sawtooth** in κ (drops upon entering each p; NUMERICAL, new); asymptotic = computational cost (N³·bits), no sign signal. κ-band conjecture (OPEN): κ₋(1−λ₄) ≤ λ^W(Λ) ≤ κ₊(1−λ₄); lower half = inverse estimate of the summation map's residual identity (quantifies C1 of the survey). μ=16 feasible (N≈2060, ≈4100 bits, ≈6 h/parity). ## Phase 200 — The sign object
- **Stage 1–2 (LEDGER_L1…L5, SPEC_SIGN_OBJECT_001):** re-analysis of RH1–RH9 and phases 0–199 in five ledgers; specification P1–P8 of the sign object (second order, not factorized, reflection/half-space positivity, reads positions of the exterior comb without going through h*, output = square/count, post-cancellation coordinates c_N). Elementary fact PROVED: c_N = −μ(N) for μ ≤ N < 2μ. Three candidates for the negative space: K1 OS-Γ, K2 DS-×ℕ (solenoid), K3 Γ-limit (Hermite, ψ_{4j}).
- **K1 OS-Γ (K1_OSG_001): NO-GO (PROVED).** Osterwalder–Schrader reflection at the edge x=T of the exterior datum FF.1 with CM kernel of poles of Γ_ℝ. The reflection identity W_T = 𝔒^∞ + 𝔇 − 𝔒^{Λ,≥μ} is FALSE: W_T is Toeplitz in Ψ̌ (thm:ext-half), window atoms pair translates by ratio N′/N ≈ n (μ=15: (22,44),(26,52),(33,66),(39,78)) and the gap c_N=0 only protects NN′ products. Exact Lemma K1.1′: reflected contribution of n<μ = Σ_{P∈[μ²,μ²n)}(c∗c)(P)P^{−½}G(log(μn/P)) ≠ 0, Möbius correlation (C6), comb-generic. 𝔒_μ not gauge-invariant (Müntz). It is the Hankel half of CA_A_007 translated to the edge (l.208 reproduced). **Restriction R-K1 (HARD): every Hankel form using the gap [2,μ) has NN′ product structure and cannot satisfy P1+P2; the sign lives in the N′/N ratio structure.** Do not retry edge reflections or CM kernels on the exterior datum.

Without renormalizing it diverges; compactness false (mass escapes to the plunge j≈μ); μ₀ from 19(b)+26 ≥ 130, ineffective. Mori μ=15: K3.1 exact (0.7306 = 0.7306, f* saturates the leak bound, ℓ ≥ d₄‖f‖²/(1+r²), r=ψ₄(0)/ψ₀(0), cte → 8/11); leading term 8.7–11.3 vs 4.08 measured. Cross block −0.481 is not reproduced at r* (−0.213/−0.238; it came from the vector p = g*₅₀); directionality confirmed. |cos∠(r*,D_n r*)| ∈ [0.06, 0.58] (< 0.6; the 0.644 of TEST_2X2 is a different normalization). θ_n (n=2,3,5): Δr_out 1e−4, 0.056, 0.34 (no mass in [λ,2λ)). 99% of ⟨Zr*,Z_log r*⟩ requires 128 classes (0.73 from order-5 class cancellation). **Same r*, different comb: LHS = 5.15 (Epstein), 6.72 (DH) — the 70-digit cancellation is in r*, not in the comb.** σ̃ lattice lines: 1e32–1e35 × r*. Not done: P4–P7. **Phase 202 closed (criterion a, R2).**
- **CALIB_R2_001 (P6, NUMERICAL ~1%): the "cancellation in the test" from CALIB_R1 was an ARTIFACT.** thm:ext-half only carries the ζ datum to the exterior (FF.1 with ζ's Möbius); substituting the comb in Z_log measures a functional different from W_X. Exterior: Δ_Epstein = +0.090e−70 (cancellation over 160 O(1) classes: 1/1 +2.47, 1/6 −1.48, 1/2 −1.46, …), Δ_DH = +1.65e−70 (82 classes; DH weights average ≈ 0 ⇒ ≈ −2·ζ-class = removing the comb). **Interior with the true g* (arb 420 bits): ⟨Λ_Q − Λ, c_{g*}⟩ = +0.0632, ⟨b^{DH} − Λ, c_{g*}⟩ = +0.0456, order O(1) (discrepancy 10⁶⁷ with the exterior); almost all from n=2: Λ_Q(2) = 0 erases the term for the prime 2 (c_{g*}(log 2) = 0.0638) — a "delete a prime" mechanism (a_P), not a cosh defect.** Pending convention B (weights a_X in Z and μ_X). Invalidates the comparison between combs in CALIB_R1 §4 and the premise of object I from phase 203.

## Phase 203 — Invent ("have it try anyway")
- **INVENT_001:** confirmation from P47 that thm:ext-half only holds for ζ (Guo's radical + Λ∗1 = log); ‖r*‖² ≍ ℓ* ≍ 1e−70 ⇒ every bounded quadratic form at r* is O(1e−70): the "near-orthogonality to every comb" is a norm effect. **Danskin (PROVED): ∂λ_A/∂a(n) = −2n^{−½}c_{g*}(log n); second variation ≤ 0 with the resolvent; Theorem 2.3 NO-GO: concavity in the comb only gives UPPER bounds on λ_A; a lower bound only from a convex combination of combs (already indexed).** Object I (cone of Euler defects over the near-common kernel 𝒩_T): NO-GO — g* ∉ 𝒩_T; ΔW_Q = +0.063 = deleting prime 2 (a_P channel); over 𝒩_T the pairing is ±Θ(ε) (PROVED to 1st order, ARGUED beyond; Lemma 13.1). Object II (radical defect by comb R_X = W_X − exterior formula of ζ with comb X): R_ζ ≡ 0 (PROVED) ⇒ NO-GO as a sign; tool/control for the CALIB_R1 artifact (L-II.1 closure to 60 digits). **Object III (hierarchical ladder of contacts): OPEN** — c_{g*}(log n), n=2..14, decays ≈ gaussianly from 6e−2 to 6e−52 ≫ W* ≈ 5e−70 ⇒ hierarchical cancellation (each prime cancels the rest at its own scale); conjecture L-esc: partial residues W^(N) ≥ 0, dominated by the next contact. New reason: fixed test (≠ lem:monoT); residue chain nonlinear in the comb (≠ Theorem X: a linear discriminant). L-III.1 (arb): 14 residues W^(N) at μ=15, sign, ratio vs. next contact. Death: some W^(N) < 0 for ζ; DH or Beurling ±1e−5 reproduce the pattern; ladder not gaussian at μ=5, 10. Risk (coordinator): if all c_{g*}(log n) > 0, W^(N) decreases toward W* and "W^(N) ≥ 0" is trivial given W* ≥ 0 (REF-D).
- **AUDIT_203_001 (adversarial): objects I, II, III NO-GO.** Danskin: gradient PROVED in the truncated class μ=15 (minimizer attained, compact, unique), CONDITIONAL on P_T; inequality (2.1) PROVED with no hypotheses; "upper bounds only, except for chords" correct (chords = convex decomposition, already indexed). **No contradiction between the cΛ line and concavity: at c=0 the value is λ_{∞,P}(T) < 0 for T ≥ 0.60 (cor:Tarch), not arch > 0; no certified negative 0.9Λ (registered falsifier: c → 0; O4ε CONDITIONAL); slope at c=1: −ΣΛ(n)s_n ≈ −0.063; the function rises from negative, peaks, drops to ≈0 at c=1. Byproduct: (1+ε)Λ with a negative background at ½log15 for ε > 8e−69 (NUMERICALLY certifiable; makes the +ε half of O4ε non-conditional at that T).** Object III: REF-D sign (c > 0 ⇒ W^(N) monotone ⇒ residues ≥ 0 ⇐ W* ≥ 0); q_N ≈ 1 is trivial from the tail and undefined at N = 5, 9, 11, 13 (Λ(N+1) = 0); the ladder = edge profile of c_{g*}, smooth in log n with no prime/composite jumps (option α, comb-generic, Theorem X); prediction: DH on its own extremal reproduces the pattern (death criterion b). New reasons not sustained ("fixed test" = partial sums of a known total; "nonlinear chain" is affine in a for each g*). II is kept as a hygiene rule (artifact control). **Phase 203 closed with no sign.**
- **CALIB_R3_001 (P203, NUMERICAL: ζ arb 420 bits, abs error ~1e−73 for tail t>736; DH mpmath 30 digits):** ζ at μ=15 with the true g*: c_{g*}(log n) > 0 ∀n=2..14 ⇒ W^(N) non-increasing: 6.32e−2, 6.23e−4, 1.09e−6, 3.85e−9, 2.15e−15 (N=5), 2.17e−19, 4.84e−23, 1.25e−31, 2.21e−43, …, 4.97791e−70 (N=14 = W_T(g*)); q_N = 0.990, 0.998, 0.996, 1−6e−7, … 1−O(1e−27); c decays faster than gaussian in log n (log₁₀c/(log n)² from 2.5 to 6.5) = edge profile (AUDIT_203). DH (witness 9/5, odd, 35 contacts): c_F has mixed signs, non-monotone ladder, first W^(N) < 0 at N=21 (−0.195‖F‖²), crossings at N=26, 29, 34, 36 (−7.05e−9). Beurling (same g*, not re-optimized): δ(log 2) = ±1e−5 ⇒ δW = ±5.29e−6; δ(log 3) ⇒ ±1.00e−7; δ = −1e−5 makes W negative (TV sensitivity ≫ 5e−70, = BARRIER_EST). P203.3 unavailable (no μ=5,10 extremals in the corpus). Confirms the AUDIT_203 prediction: comb-generic, Theorem X. **Phases 202–203 closed.**

- **AUDIT_204_H1MINUS_001: H1⁻ PROVED (arbitrary-precision intervals, 120 bits):** 𝔞_{0.12}(χ_ev)/‖χ_ev‖² ∈ [−0.047 ± 3.5e−4] (c_χ(u) = sinh(T−u/2) + (T−u/2)cosh(u/2), c(0) = sinh T + T = ‖χ‖²; comb ≤ 0; C₀ = ψ(¼) − log π, K = lem:K, tail log coth(T/2) + 2arctan e^{−T} = thm:S(iii) closed; piece [0,1e−12] contained) ⇒ by zero-extension (lem:monoT) + min–max, n₋(𝔄_ev(T)) ≥ 1 ∀T ≥ 0.12. Excludes the n₋ = 0 branch of prop:WT: **for T ≥ 0.12, even sector positive ⟺ n₋(𝔄_ev) = 1 ∧ q_ev < −½. P47: cor:H1minus after prop:WT (190 pp; backup main_pre_h1minus.tex).** Does not give n₋ = 1 nor the sign of q_ev + ½ (NUMERICAL). Certificates at 0.5 and ½log3 are redundant.
- **H1_TRACT_001 (Phase 204r): H1⁺ and 𝔄_odd ≻ 0 → TRANSFER, OPEN.** (A) H1⁺ necessary not sufficient (D ⇒ WP^ev ⇒ H1⁺ PROVED; a_P at ½log3 satisfies H1⁺ and fails D). **Lemma A∞ (PROVED, count ARGUED): the archimedean form alone has n₋ → ∞ (≈ 4T directions; m < 0 in |τ| < 6.29 CITED) ⇒ H1⁺ and 𝔄_odd ≻ 0 FALSE for v = 0 and for θΛ with θ < θ_c(T): the comb must cancel all but one of the archimedean negative directions (Λ-specific).** Class NO-GO (PROVED): Perron–Frobenius/Markov/P60-1 do not reach it (θΛ satisfies their hypotheses; P60-1 gives simplicity of the background, not the sign of the 2nd eigenvalue). Second eigenvalue ∈ 𝒦_W (CITED BARRIER_W), 𝒦_H ARGUED. Class: (N_loc)/K34 in codimension 1 = class of (L2). Input if it were proved: with H1⁻, D^ev ⟺ lim q_ev ≤ −½ with q monotone (scalar REF-D by Prop S). Status: PROVED T ≤ ½log5; CONDITIONAL D0 in (½log5, ½log15]; OPEN after. Closeable without D: Lemma A∞ with explicit constants.
- **AUDIT_204_CSTAR_001: (C★) NO-GO as E∧F composition — IT CHAINS (PROVED from COMPOSE §5.2 and prop:ext-gauge).** In Λ, b ≡ 1 and 2δ_E(Λ;f)² = ℓ(Ef) ⇒ E enters as identity (Q-P2.2 does not define δ_E at ζ, only a rate ≈ 4.5e5 at ½log3); term F = fixed seminorm ‖Lf‖² (L universal linear, Σ_k(f(km) − f̂(km))/log m) with sign −, does not distinguish ζ; F only contributes 𝔇_F(Λ) = 0. Content with sign: W_T ≥ ℓ in ker L = prob:ext-L/eq:ext-starstar (κ=1, A₀=0) restricted; |s(A)| = 1; abstract falsifier 𝒲′ = ℓ − ε‖·‖² (same form as Lemma R ∧ gap). BARRIER_D1 Prop 1 answers risk 1 (tangent {0}); "coercive at a = good" is ill-posed; Kronecker does not apply; F is on the summation side (not Σ_ρ) but W_T ≥ ℓ in ker L would be REF-D. "Cancellation up to 1e−7" is not a prediction (one-sided bound, K free; confuses Galerkin 𝔤 9e−8 and q_ev+½ = −2.16e−8 with W_T(h_ev)). No requests to the calibrator. **"Only construction with genuine composition form" is withdrawn: a real composition would use 𝔇_F at a ≠ Λ in a sign step nonlinear in a — today no such object exists.** Do not retry "F as derivative of Poisson at Λ."
- **LEMMA_AINF_001 (Phase 204s): Theorem A∞ PROVED with constants, without Slepian.** 𝔪_T(F) = (1/2π)∫m|F̂|² (eq:Aform with v ≡ 0): n₋(𝔪_T) ≥ N(T) = ⌈2Tt₀/π⌉ − 1 ≈ 4.0042·T (error ≤ 1); by sector even ≥ ⌈N/2⌉, odd ≥ ⌊N/2⌋; margin λ_N ≤ −δ_N, δ_N = B(t₁² − (πN/2T)²) − m(t₁), B = φ′(t₁²) (B = 0.012665 at t₀). Proof: φ concave (thm:S) ⇒ m(τ) ≤ m(t₁) + B(τ² − t₁²); the logarithmic tail is absorbed into ‖F′‖², diagonal in the Dirichlet basis sin(πk(x+T)/2T) (k odd = even, k even = odd). t₀ = 6.289836 NUMERICAL (certifiable); with t₁ = 3 fully PROVED version, N ≈ 1.91T. **Corollary C (necessity PROVED): n₋(𝔪_T + R) ≥ #{j : μ_j(R_N) < δ_N}; H1⁺ requires n₊ ≥ ⌈N(T)/2⌉ − 1 of the comb in an explicit space of dimension ⌈N(T)/2⌉ — the sign is a cancellation of ≍ 4T archimedean negative directions by the comb, not a perturbative smallness.** Galerkin (48 Legendre): n₋ = N(T) exact for T ∈ {½log3, 1, ½log15, 2, 3} = (2, 4, 5, 8, 12), also by sector; upper bound OPEN. Epstein T=2 CONDITIONAL (reading n₋ = 2 from CALIB_R4). **P47: lem:archcount after thm:A (190 pp; backup main_pre_archcount.tex).**
- **H1PLUS_001 (Phase 204t): H1⁺ as an independent route NO-GO (REF-D by P4).** Exact identity (PROVED; eq:W, lem:roughEF, prop:WT): for real even F in 𝒟_T, 𝔄_ev(F) = Z_T(F) − 2⟨F,χ_ev⟩², Z_T = Σ_ρ Φ_F(ρ)² = W^ev_T (zero form); no prime tail (c_F(log n) = 0 for n ≥ e^{2T}); no boundary term (κ rewrites the archimedean term; lem:roughEF covers rough tests); σ is not a multiplier (zeros outside and pole = evaluation of F̂ at complex points). **Inertia count (PROVED): n₋(Z_T) ≤ n₋(𝔄_ev) ≤ n₋(Z_T) + 1; zero on the line ⇒ |F̂(γ)|² ≥ 0; quadruple off the line ⇒ hyperbolic pair (1,1) ⇒ n₋(𝔄_ev) ≤ 1 + N_hip.** CALIB fits (NUMERICAL): ζ's "1" = subtracted polar direction; Epstein T=2 n₋ = 2 = pole + one hyperbolic pair; DH without pole without "+1". **Proposition H: H1⁺ ∀T ⟺ W^ev ⪰ 0 ∀T = D^ev (⇐ PROVED; ⇒ ARGUED via boundary-concentration Lemma LCB) — H1⁺ is not a "half" of D^ev** (corrects H1_TRACT/WT_HALVES: "necessary not sufficient" holds per window, not ∀T; prediction ARGUED: a_P violates H1⁺ at large T via "zeros" at Re s = 0). No mechanism without zeros (NO-GO): in frequency the residual is E(x) = ψ(x) − x at scale x^{1/2}; Chebyshev/dlVP give neither size nor sign; bound on E = zero side. Closeable OPENs without D: Lemma LCB with constants (⇒ Prop H PROVED); PRED-aP: n₋ ≥ 2 for a_P in the CALIB pipeline (if it fails, LCB is wrong and H1⁺ returns to class (L2)).

## Phase 205 — The object that composes E and F
- Mandate: build the object whose sign step uses Euler (Dirichlet series over ℕ) and the FE (conductor 1) at the same time, composed (Def O1.2), not chained; skeleton → audit → pieces one by one. Sign architecture (PROVED phase 204): count ≍ 4T archimedean negative directions (lem:archcount) of which the comb cancels all but one (cor:H1minus; prop:WT); residual inertia = pole + hyperbolic pairs (H1PLUS). **Starting point (new): in E_N = span{Dirichlet sines, k ≤ N ≈ 4T}, even positivity reduces to the inertia of the finite EXPLICIT matrix C_N(k,k′) = −Σ_{n ≤ e^{2T}} v_n⟨s_k, τ_{log n}s_{k′}⟩ ≈ Toeplitz−Hankel of the partial sum of the symbol σ_T at frequencies πk/2T; T(m − σ_T) − H(m − σ_T) ≽ 0 in E_N except for one direction, with m − σ_T = comb tail beyond e^{2T} (the exterior in finite frequency coordinates).** In progress: SKELETON_001 (architect), CALIB_R5 (explicit matrices at μ=15, Epstein, a_P), TOOLS_204U (LCB, PRED-aP, A∞ upper bound).
- **SKELETON_001 (architect, Phase 205): skeleton without a composed sign step yet; 3 tools PROVED (0 prior hits):** (P2a) comb matrix in Dirichlet sines G_kl(u) = ⟨τ_u e_l, e_k⟩ with elementary closed formula (Toeplitz−Hankel reading of the sampled symbol only approximate, ARGUED); **(P3) Green's function of the pole: in the even sector, the principal part of the comb (density 2e^{u/2}, residue 1) satisfies M(F) = E_G(F) − β_T⟨F,χ_ev⟩², E_G = ∫(g′² + ¼g²) ≥ 0 with g = −sinh(|·|/2)∗F, β_T = 1 + ½sinh T (e^{|x−y|/2} = rank 2 in e^{±x/2} + Green's function of −∂²+¼) ⇒ "all but one direction" is an exact identity;** coefficients of χ_ev in sines c_k = 2ω_k cosh(T/2)/(√T(¼+ω_k²)) > 0 (91.2% of χ in E_5^ev at Mori). Corrections: comb tail empty in P_T (H1PLUS §4.2); **pointwise criterion m − σ_T ≥ 0 FALSE (NO-GO PROVED: at τ=0 ≈ C₀ − 4e^T)**; 𝔪_⊥ ≥ 0 not automatic (sines not band-limited = A∞ upper bound OPEN); Piece 1 only necessity (n₋(𝔄_N^ev) ≤ 1; Schur per frequency exact, 𝔄_⊥ ≻ 0 OPEN); "composes" only trivially (compressed objective). Core: 𝔅_T = 𝔪 + E_G + Φ_E (Φ_E = form of ψ(t) − t); with H1PLUS (1.4): 𝔅_T = Z_T + (½sinh T − 1)ℓ² (CONDITIONAL normalizations) = zero form + rank-one polar slack (REF-D). Piece 4: falsifiers in the identity 𝔪^a + r_a(E_G − βℓ²) + Φ^a (a_P: explicit matrix; DH without polar; Beurling/quartets: undefined skeleton; frequency of Epstein's hyperbolic pair OPEN). **Only live node L205.★ (OPEN, no candidate): 𝔪 + E_G + Φ_E ≥ 0 on E_N^ev ∩ ker ℓ with a nonlinear step at Λ using F; death K1 (bounds on E(x)), K2 (reduction to zeros), K3 (linear at Λ), K4 (FE as trace), K5 (common falsifier), K6 (Mori).** Order: Piece 5 (calibrator, 3×3 Mori, prediction n₋ = 1, δ_5 ≈ 0.075) → P3 even/odd into P47 → P1.c → L205.★.
- **AUDIT_205_SKELETON_001 (adversarial): the skeleton has no sign node.** P3 correct (β_T = 1 + ½sinh T even; β′_T = ½sinh T − 1 odd, PROVED) **but = REWRITE of P47 lem:polarelim extended outside P_T**: canonical form (R1) M(F) = 𝔢(F) − 2(⟨F,ch⟩² − ⟨F,sh⟩²) over all of L²(I_T), 𝔢 = (1/2π)∫|F̂|²/(¼+τ²); E_G = 𝔢 + (½sinh T − 1)ℓ² (not canonical; "symbol 1/(¼+τ²)" belongs to 𝔢). **Φ_E = −𝓕_T from prop:sharp = −R_Λ from PROOF_L_SHARP_B Lemma 3.1** (vs V_001 only moves ½log(1−e^{−2t}) elsewhere): REF-D already indexed. (6.1) PROVED given (1.4) with no normalization condition (the two "2"s are the same pole); slack ½sinh T − 1 = boundary-energy artifact, with no content. **L205.★ WITHDRAWN (REF-D from the statement): ker ℓ ∩ L²_ev = P_T^ev ⇒ 𝔅_T = 𝔞^♯ − 𝓕_T = 𝔞_T; = prop:sharp(ii) compressed to E_N^ev ∩ P_T; K2; necessity only.** Piece 1 composes only trivially; **P1.c (𝔄_⊥ ≻ 0 on the complement) = the only OPEN piece that is not REF-D.** NO-GO m − σ_T ≥ 0 correct but redundant (cor:H1minus). - **CALIB_R5_001 (Piece 5, NUMERICAL 40 digits, 200 nodes):** closed formula for c_{kk′}(u) (a = πk/2T, b = πk′/2T, L = 2T − u; a ≠ b: ½[(sin((a−b)L − bu) + sin(bu))/(a−b) − (sin((a+b)L + bu) − sin(bu))/(a+b)]; a = b analogous), error 1e−40; **EXACT Toeplitz−Hankel reading (Toeplitz in τ_k − τ_{k′}, Hankel in τ_k + τ_{k′}) with phases ±b·log n.** ζ: n₋(𝔪_N) = N(T) exact at 6 values of T (⌈N/2⌉ even, ⌊N/2⌋ odd); **n₋(𝔄_ev) = 1 at all T and at N, 2N, 4N: the cancellation of N − 1 directions occurs WITHIN E_N (no complement needed); negative eigenvector = polar direction (cos(v_min, χ_ev) ≥ 0.998, ≥ 0.9999 at N(T)); λ_min(𝔄_ev) = −1.81 (½log3), −5.77 (½log15), −23.8 (T=3);** 𝔄_odd ≻ 0 with decreasing background in N; eigenvalues ~1e−15 at 2N/4N = quadrature noise. **Epstein (its Γ): n₋ = 1 up to ½log15; T=2: even → 2 only with 4N (−1.47) + odd −1.05; T=3: 1 → 2 → 3; the hyperbolic pair appears only with sines k > N(T) (frequency > t₀).** **a_P (P={2}): no cancellation — n₋(𝔄_ev) ≥ n₋(𝔪), grows with N (T=3: 5, 9, 16); odd background −0.30…−0.57.** Not done: sign of the 1e−15 values; exact k of the inertia change at Epstein/a_P (band (N, 4N]); DH. **Reading: the object in finite coordinates = N×N matrix with symbol m − σ_T; the peak ≈ 4e^T at τ ≈ 0 (main term ψ ~ x) = the only negative direction (polar); the rest (fluctuation ψ(x) − x in frequency) must be dominated by m in the Toeplitz sense — exactly the half with no mechanism without zeros (M1/K1).**
- **TOOLS_204U_001 (Phase 204u): three tools closed without D.** **(1) LCB′ PROVED ⇒ Prop H PROVED: n₋(𝔄_ev(T)) ≤ 1 ∀T ⟺ W^ev_T ⪰ 0 ∀T** (if W_{T₀}(G) < 0, in span{G, χ_T} det 𝔄_ev = ℓ(χ_T)²(−2W(G) + o(1)) by polarized lem:roughEF, 0 < Re ρ < 1, Σ|ρ|^{−2} < ∞, lem:monoT; explicit threshold T₁ (1.1)); with cor:H1minus: **D^ev ⟺ the pole-free even form has exactly one negative eigenvalue ∀T. P47: prop:H after cor:H1minus (190 pp; backup main_pre_propH.tex).** Strong version n₋ → 1 + N_hip OPEN (quadruples with larger β not uniformly dominable; sign flips). **(2) PRED-aP NUMERICAL confirmed** (M=40: erasing {2}: n₋(𝔄_ev) = 1, 2, 4, 9, 9, 10; {2,3}: 1, 2, 2, 8, 9, 9; ζ: always 1; Galerkin = lower bounds); **mechanism PROVED distinct from H1PLUS: 𝔄^{a_P} = 𝔄^ζ + B_P, B_P a multiplier uniformly bounded in T (≥ −0.5742 for {2}, ≥ −1.3785 for {2,3}; data saturate: −0.5705, −1.3049 at T=3) — the almost-kernel of W^ζ acts.** LCB does not fail ⇒ H1⁺ does not return to (L2). Artifact: ζ T=3 M=40 λ^W ≈ −4.6e−40 = precision. **(3) A∞ upper bound PROVED: n₋(𝔪_T) ≤ ⌈2Tt₀/π⌉ + 8 + (2/π²)log(16Tt₁+25)·log(170T), t₁ = t₀ + 1/T (minorant step + prolate count; Landau 1993, Karnik–Romberg–Davenport ACHA 2021 CITED — constants from memory, verify before P47) ⇒ n₋(𝔪_T) = 2Tt₀/π + O(log²T); exact O(1) OPEN (plunge; 2nd-order Szegő with log tail).** Dirichlet sines do not close the upper bound (m grows only logarithmically).
- **INTEGRATION_205_001: P2a/CALIB_R5 verified and integrated into P47 (191 pp, 0 errors; backup main_pre_205.tex):** lem:combsine (PROVED) after lem:archcount: exact closed formula G_kl(u) (30-digit quadrature, error ≤ 5e−32), G_lk = (−1)^{k+l}G_kl ⇒ even/odd sectors decoupled; EXACT comb matrix in D_T(ω) = Σv_n sin(ω log n) and C_T (Fejér-cosine on the diagonal): Toeplitz part = divided difference of D_T, Hankel part = (D_k + D_l)/(ω_k + ω_l), exact phases. **Correction to CALIB_R5: "the Toeplitz part depends only on the frequency difference" is inexact (the numerators depend on each frequency individually).** rem:chiev-sine (c_k > 0, Σc_k² = T + sinh T), rem:finitesign (NUMERICAL, cites CALIB_R5/TOOLS_204U), rem:polarelim-full ((R1) extended to 𝒟_T: W_T = 𝔞^♯ − 𝓕_T). Not integrated: A∞ upper bound (constants pending), t₀′, E_G/β_T, 𝔅_T, L205.★.
- **RFIN_TRACT_001 (adversarial reviewer, Phase 205): finite reformulation "n₋(𝔄_N(T)) = 1 ∀T" (Dirichlet sines, N(T) ≈ 4T) — TRACTABLE = NO; NO-GO as a route (REF-D in block, N3).** Necessity PROVED (and stronger: Z_N ≽ 0 on all of E_N); chain Z_N ≽ 0 ⇒ L205.★ ⇒ n₋(𝔄_N) ≤ 1 (PROVED); converse with fixed N(T) requires P1.c′ (OPEN); "∀T ∀N" = D^ev by prop:H. It is D with the tail cut off: it fully preserves the work of cancelling ≈ N − 1 directions. **Szegő paradox resolved: the main term of the compressed comb is exactly 𝔢 − 2ccᵀ (exact rank one by (R1)), not a symbol; identity RF.1 (PROVED-elementary): 𝔄_N = (𝔪+𝔢)_N − 2ccᵀ + Φ_N, n₋(𝔄_N) ≤ 1 + n₋(Z_N), budget n₊(Φ_N) ≥ ⌈N′/2⌉ − 2 ≈ 1.95T − 2.** Szegő/Widom/Avram–Parter/GLT do not apply: Z_N ≽ 0 is averaged symbol positivity with sincs of width π/T while σ^E_T oscillates at scale 1/T with support at u = window (critical sampling); the phase −b·log n makes the error relative to a full-rank Toeplitz (Prop RF.2 = W-det/R164_502c). Necessary size O(1) positive in ≈1.95T directions: without zeros only dlVP e^{T−c√T} with no sign ⇒ M1. Difference from N3 only computational (dim ≈ 2T vs k(T) exponential); the cancellation within the block leaves a margin ~1e−15 (analogue of the 1e−70 of (L2)). Discarded: Gram arithmetic identity (K2/Kronecker), rank (full-rank truncated translation), continuation in T (𝒦_W, L2_INTERLACE), Cauchy interlacing (wrong direction). RF.3 (ARGUED): non-band-limited sines ⇒ Epstein's hyperbolic pair weighs e^{(2β−1)T} and enters E_N for T ≳ T_ρ; decidable with calibrator P5′ (Epstein at N(T), T = 4…7). Do not retry the finite reformulation without a bound on Φ_N independent of zeros.

## Phase 206 — The node (step 4) and the E∧F rigidity family
- Mandate: (A) build the node of the mechanism's skeleton (phase README: 4 PROVED steps + OPEN node Φ_N(F) ≥ −(𝔪+𝔢)(F) on the polar orthogonal complement, forced properties (i)–(v), dead mechanisms); (B) objects where E and F coexist nonlinearly (reciprocals, RS, theta), quadratic version of rigidity.
- **RIGID_001 (Phase 206, track B, constructor, no audit; new reason: nonlinear E∧F objects with global F that were not
  in the index):** catalogue of 12 objects (Hecke/Weil/Hamburger reciprocals, Bochner–Hecke, θ, Eisenstein/MS,
  RS, metaplectic, Voronoi, Kuznetsov/Salié, Estermann, Kronecker–Hurwitz, Helson); 8 RETRY. New: **Lemma V**

## Phase 207 — Class impossibility and the empty-cell object
- Mandate (author): (1) class impossibility theorem for the node; (2) invent the object for the cell "weight n^{−1/2} + global FE + built-in positivity."

Helson PROVED: ∫_{½}^∞|Σa_n n^{−σ}|²dσ = Σ a_m ā_n/(√(mn)log(mn)) (without the factor 2 from RIGID §2.12) = integral over a fundamental domain of σ ↦ 1−σ, symmetric extension diverges ⇒ no FE by construction. Prop 1.5 PROVED: ⟨f,Jf⟩ = ‖P₊f‖² − ‖P₋f‖²; every J-invariant form ≥ 0 is an average of forms ≥ 0 ⇒ the FE as mere symmetry contributes no sign. Lemma 3.2 PROVED: no nonzero Dirichlet polynomial lies in the ±1 eigenspaces of J. **Lemma G (PROVED given Gonek CITED): ⟨D_a, JD_a⟩_w = 2π Σ_N w(2πN)(a∗a)(N) + error — the FE part of every weight-½ form is the Dirichlet convolution over N = mn (e(mn) = 1: native (ℕ,+,·) interaction); with full Euler a∗a = a·d and it remains linear in a.** Theorem C (algebra PROVED, stationary phase ARGUED) in the class B = M_f + M_g J: every sign step is a generic norm (Lemma N), linear in Λ (Theorem X), or has coefficients ≥ 0; none separates a_P or cΛ. Candidates: (1) Helson with involution/quadratic Hamburger defect = RETRY (BARRIER_D1 Thm 4); (2) split Hardy form ‖Re e^{iθ}D‖² − ‖Im e^{iθ}D‖² NO-GO ARGUED (new); Z², Z·G = RETRY G3C/R163_603; (3) prime theta with RS NO-GO ARGUED (without automorphy there is no unfolding; m+n only with coefficients ≥ 0); compression of J to X-friable NO-GO PROVED given Lemma G (indefinite; full form = C_006 E4); completely multiplicative variant NO-GO ARGUED (Liouville). **OPEN: O1 non-local forms in t outside ⟨M_f, J⟩ (the node lives there: its support compression is not J-stable); O2 degree ≥ 3 positivities / multiplicative Hankel matrices.** Necessary condition for a future object: non-local in t and using J. Row D OPEN.

- **CLASS_001 (Phase 207, track 1, constructor, no audit):** class impossibility theorem for the sign step of node 𝒩_T. New reason: the object is the node (a finite LMI in v_n, n < e^{2T}), not λ_A/W; it quantifies over all Ψ.

## Phase 208: scope of the node — consolidated audit

`03-research/phase-208-node-scope-audit/README.md`. No new sign route nor
reopening. CITED corrections from 206–207: ZCERT only grid points up to
4.245, not the interval T≤4.25; block N(T) does not substitute for the full
domain (P1.c′ OPEN); even sector does not substitute for all of D; count only
necessary; μ_N≥μ_∞. Elementary reminder PROVED, not a new mechanism: Λ*1=log
characterizes Λ without zeros; removing prime powers leaves the defect
v_p(n)log p. Hence "no arithmetic separator" must be read as "no proven
arithmetic implication of sign." Removing only p>e^{2T} does not change that
window: global completeness and the sign of a window have different
quantifiers. Möbius/E.2/Selberg/Gram are not reopened. No current theorem proves
that every Euler product other than ζ violates the node, nor that no other
bridge can exist. No change in grid states; general node and row D OPEN.

  association. Not counted as a polar-free mechanism: the origin contributes an

  of τ=θ−1 with analytic/Poisson proof and rational constants. For ALL

**Precise residue:** diagonal/same-prime renormalized, global sum,

### Phase 209 — active goal D and canonical completion of the kernel

The active goal **complete the full proof of D** was explicitly established;
recorded as active, with no artificial budget. State in GOAL_D_STATUS.md.

- **CONSTRUCTOR_005 / AUDIT_005 (PROVED):** for 0<α<1/2, s=2α,
  κ=−2π^{−α}Γ(α)ζ(2α)>0, the kernel −Rα already defined by subtracting cG₁ is
  **SPD on c_00 of distinct divisors ≥2**. Exact identity
  −Rα(A)=κ E_s|A−μ₁(A)|²+∫y^{α−1}τ|E_yA−μ₁(A)|²dy.
  First term: Mellin of the signed defect cE₁−τE_y in the finite algebra
  of divisibility, which is κE_s. Pointwise positivity of that
  defect is not required. P_s is the law of finitely many valuations, not the full zeta law at s<1.
- Explicit kernel: κ[G_s+v_s v_s*]+Π_α, with
  G_s=lcm^{−s}−(de)^{−s}, v_s=d^{−s}−d^{−1}, Π a Gaussian Gram matrix centered
  on the Haar mean. Diagonal and same-prime terms paid for THIS R; not for Q_T.
- **Independent same-prime control (PROVED):** Gaussian shells
  τ(p^{2j}y)−τ(p^{2j+2}y)<c(1−1/p)p^{−j}; comparison of variance minima
  gives cG₁−B⪰0 in each pointwise block. Do not extrapolate to all primes.
- **Finite inheritance (PROVED):** when deleting P, referencing δ_Pc, positive factor
  κ_P=κ∏(1−p^{−s}), law P_s conditioned on v_p=0. PSD kernel, SPD only on
  surviving independent indicators. AUXILIARY PSD not specific;
  the link to Q must use an additional premise that fails for the falsifier.
- **Controls:** D_y=cG₁−B_y can be indefinite at several primes before
  Mellin. Primorial sieve counterexample in AUDIT005§8. Does not contradict the
  integrated theorem. mpmath 35dps diagnostics in DIAGONAL_005_data motivated the
  conjecture; they are exploratory and the analytic proof replaces them.

A generic Gram matrix is not reopened as a certificate: what is new is the identity with
the renormalized R FIXED, not choosing a favorable diagonal or root.
Do not attribute that sign to Q_T. Work006 in progress on the specific map/Poisson.
O1 and D OPEN, goal active; no closure nor integration into P47.
